ops-platform         | prisma:query 
ops-platform         |       SELECT DISTINCT cr.published_skill_id, cr.release_version
ops-platform         |       FROM capability_releases cr
ops-platform         |       INNER JOIN deployment_records dr ON dr.release_id = cr.id
ops-platform         |       WHERE cr.status = 'published'
ops-platform         |         AND cr.deployment_status IN ('deployed', 'succeeded', 'completed')
ops-platform         |         AND dr.status IN ('deployed', 'succeeded', 'completed')
ops-platform         |         AND dr.success = true
